Despite my wife and all those doctors trying to tell me I'm crazy, I know what I feel in my shoulder and I've spent months gathering the proof in my workshop. Why would I question it when the physical sensation and their cover-up are so obvious?\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if there have been any times when your belief about the implant was not present, or if it has been constant since the shoulder scan nine months ago?\n\nPatient: It has been completely constant every single day since that scan nine months ago.
